The user wants me to analyze the provided specification text (from priv-intro.adoc, lines 1-217) and extract all architectural parameters. I need to identify parameters that are defined in this text and extract them with their details.

Let me analyze the text carefully for architectural parameters. The text covers:
1. Introduction to RISC-V privileged architecture
2. Privileged Software Stack Terminology (ABI, SBI, HBI, AEE, SEE, HEE)
3. Privilege Levels (U, S, M modes)
4. Debug Mode

Looking for architectural parameters - these are typically things like:
- Privilege levels and their encodings
- Number of privilege levels supported
- Mandatory vs optional privilege levels
- Debug mode existence
- CSR addresses reserved for debug mode
- Physical address space reservations for debug

Let me go through the text line by line and identify architectural parameters.

From the text:

1. **Privilege Levels** (lines 77-95):
   - Three privilege levels defined: U (00), S (01), Reserved (10), M (11)
   - Encodings: 00, 01, 10, 11
   - Names: User/Application, Supervisor, Reserved, Machine
   - Abbreviations: U, S, (none), M

2. **Mandatory privilege level** (line 97): "The machine level has the highest privileges and is the only mandatory privilege level for a RISC-V hardware platform."

3. **Supported combinations of privilege modes** (lines 107-117):
   - 1 level: M only (Simple embedded systems)
   - 2 levels: M, U (Secure embedded systems)
   - 3 levels: M, S, U (Systems running Unix-like OSs)
   - "All hardware implementations must provide M-mode" (line 119)

4. **Debug Mode** (lines 155-163):
   - Debug mode (D-mode) may be included
   - Considered an additional privilege mode with more access than M-mode
   - Reserves a few CSR addresses only accessible in D-mode
   - May reserve portions of physical address space

Let me identify the architectural parameters from this text:

1. **Privilege Levels** - The defined privilege levels and their encodings
2. **Number of Privilege Levels Supported** - 1, 2, or 3 levels (M only, M+U, M+S+U)
3. **Mandatory Privilege Level** - M-mode is mandatory
4. **Debug Mode** - Optional debug mode (D-mode)
   - Debug mode CSR addresses reserved
   - Debug mode physical address space reservation
